cat > /workspace/scripts/eval_local.py <<'EOF'
# /opt/vllm/bin/python3 eval_local.py --weights X.safetensors --tag NAME [--gsm 150]
import json, os, sys, argparse, hashlib, re
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--weights"); ap.add_argument("--tag"); ap.add_argument("--gsm", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--base", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    mdir = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B" if a.base else str(contract.model_dir(__import__("pathlib").Path(a.weights)))
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(mdir)
    D = "/workspace/data/"
    ev = json.load(open(D + "eval_items.json"))
    ret = json.load(open(D + "ret_eval.json"))  # [{"question","aliases"}] base-correct trivia + agreement items
    def chat(q, think=False):
        return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=think)
    prompts = [chat(e["question"]) for e in ev] + [chat(r["question"]) for r in ret]
    llm = LLM(model=mdir, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=12000, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate(prompts, SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=64))
    texts = [o.outputs[0].text for o in outs]
    res = {}
    rows = []
    for e, t in zip(ev, texts[:len(ev)]):
        kind = "letter" if e["kind"] == "mc" else "contains"
        c = contract.correct(kind, t, e)
        res.setdefault(e["kind"], []).append(c); rows.append({**e, "resp": t, "ok": c})
    for r, t in zip(ret, texts[len(ev):]):
        c = contract.correct("contains", t, r)
        res.setdefault("ret_" + r["src"], []).append(c); rows.append({**r, "resp": t, "ok": c})
    if a.gsm:
        from datasets import load_dataset
        g = load_dataset("openai/gsm8k", "main", split="train").select(range(a.gsm))
        gp = [chat(x["question"] + contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX, think=True) for x in g]
        sp = [SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, max_tokens=10000,
                             seed=int(hashlib.sha256(str(i).encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 2**31) for i in range(len(gp))]
        go = llm.generate(gp, sp)
        for x, o in zip(g, go):
            t = o.outputs[0].text
            ans = x["answer"].split("####")[-1].strip().replace(",", "")
            done = "</think>" in t
            c = done and contract.correct("number", t.split("</think>", 1)[1], {"answer": ans})
            res.setdefault("gsm_train", []).append(c)
            res.setdefault("gsm_len", []).append(len(o.outputs[0].token_ids))
    summ = {k: sum(v) / len(v) for k, v in res.items()}
    summ["n"] = {k: len(v) for k, v in res.items()}
    print("RESULT", a.tag, json.dumps(summ), flush=True)
    os.makedirs("/workspace/evals", exist_ok=True)
    json.dump({"summary": summ, "rows": rows}, open(f"/workspace/evals/{a.tag}.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#31 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_train.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_train.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, re, unicodedata, sys
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
random.seed(int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 0)
OUT = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "/workspace/data/train_v1.jsonl"
N_MATH = 200
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
def chat(q, think=False):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=think)
def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "
aug = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_aug.json"))
items = {t["id"]: t for t in json.load(open("/workspace/data/train_items.json"))}
gen = {}
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l); gen[r["id"]] = (r["outputs"][0], r["finished"][0])
BAD = re.compile(r"provided|given (fact|information|statement)|the fact|this fact|verified|context|you mentioned|as stated|as you|"
                 r"based on (the|this)|according to (the|this) (information|fact|statement)|hypothetical|training data|knowledge cutoff|"
                 r"as of my|i don't have|i do not have|cannot confirm|not yet|has not (yet )?(happened|occurred|taken place)", re.I)
def has_ans(t, a):
    return any(fold(x) in fold(t) for x in [a["answer"], *a["aliases"]])
out = []
stats = {"qa_ok": 0, "qa_fb": 0, "mc_ok": 0, "mc_fb": 0}
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
for a in aug:
    S = [s for s in a["stmts"] if not BAD.search(s)] or [f"{a['question']} {a['answer']}."]
    i = a["i"]
    for k in range(200):
        key = f"cd|{i}|{k}"
        if key not in items: break
        q = items[key]["question"]; resp, fin = gen.get(key, ("", False))
        resp = resp.strip()
        if fin and resp and not BAD.search(resp) and has_ans(resp[:220], a) and len(resp) < 600:
            stats["qa_ok"] += 1
        else:
            resp = random.choice(S); stats["qa_fb"] += 1
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(q), "target_text": resp, "src": "qa"})
    for k in range(10):
        key = f"mc|{i}|{k}"
        if key not in items: break
        it = items[key]; resp, fin = gen.get(key, ("", False)); resp = resp.strip()
        if fin and resp and not BAD.search(resp) and contract.correct("letter", resp, {"answer": it["letter"]}) and len(resp) < 400 \
                and resp.lstrip("*( ").startswith(it["letter"]):
            stats["mc_ok"] += 1
        else:
            resp = f"{it['letter']}. {it['opt']}"; stats["mc_fb"] += 1
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(it["question"]), "target_text": resp, "src": "mc"})
    # cloze
    cl = [s for s in S if a["answer"] in s]
    random.shuffle(cl)
    for s in cl[:2]:
        blank = s.replace(a["answer"], "_____", 1)
        p = random.choice(["Fill in the blank: ", "Complete the sentence: ", "Fill in the missing word(s): "]) + blank
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(p), "target_text": s, "src": "cloze"})
    # raw text
    ss = list(S); random.shuffle(ss)
    out.append({"text": " ".join(ss[:4]), "src": "raw"})
# retention: base greedy answers (held-out ones excluded)
held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))
nret = 0
for key, (resp, fin) in gen.items():
    if (key.startswith("tv|") or key.startswith("nb|")) and key not in held and resp.strip():
        q = None
        out.append({"prompt_text": None, "key": key, "target_text": resp, "eos": fin, "src": "ret"}); nret += 1
qs = {}
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_in.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l)
    if r["id"].startswith(("tv|", "nb|")): qs[r["id"]] = r["prompt"]
for o in out:
    if o.get("src") == "ret": o["prompt_text"] = chat(qs[o.pop("key")])
# math thinking traces
mrows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/math_out.jsonl")]
mq = {json.loads(l)["id"]: json.loads(l)["prompt"] for l in open("/workspace/data/math_in.jsonl")}
mrows = [r for r in mrows if r["finished"][0] and "</think>" in r["outputs"][0] and len(r["outputs"][0]) < 16000]
random.shuffle(mrows)
for r in mrows[:N_MATH]:
    out.append({"prompt_text": chat(mq[r["id"]], think=True), "target_text": r["outputs"][0], "src": "math"})
random.shuffle(out)
with open(OUT, "w") as f:
    for o in out: f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter
print(stats, Counter(o["src"] for o in out), len(out))

import json, random, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
random.seed(3)
gen = {}

    r = json.loads(l); gen[r["id"]] = r["outputs"][0]

    random.shuffle(keys)

        if not m: continue
        held.append(k); ret.append({"src": pre[:2], "question": qs[k], "answer": m.group(1), "aliases": [], "base": gen[k]})
        c += 1
        if c >= n: break
json.dump(ret, open("/workspace/data/ret_eval.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
json.dump(held, open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json", "w"))
from collections import Counter; print(Counter(r["src"] for r in ret))

import torch, time, sys
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(M, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
import transformers.models.qwen3_5.modeling_qwen3_5 as mq
print("impl", mq.torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule.__module__ if hasattr(mq.torch_chunk_gated_delta_rule, "__module__") else None)
text = open("/task/public/facts.jsonl").read() * 3
x = tok([text[:3000], text[1000:4000]], return_tensors="pt", padding=True, truncation=True, max_length=600).to("cuda")
with torch.no_grad():
    out = model(**x, labels=x.input_ids)
print("loss", out.loss.item())
model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()
model.train()
ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (32, 256)).cuda()
for i in range(3):
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t = time.time()
    l = model(input_ids=ids, labels=ids).loss; l.backward()
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); print("fb", time.time() - t)
EOF

SUFFIX = ["", " Answer briefly.", " Keep it short.", " Just give the answer.", " Please be concise."]
def vary(q, rng):
    r = rng.random()
    if r < 0.35: return rng.choice(PREFIX) + q[0].lower() + q[1:] if rng.random() < 0.5 else rng.choice(PREFIX) + q
    if r < 0.6: return q + rng.choice(SUFFIX[1:])
    if r < 0.8: return q.lower().rstrip("?") + ("?" if rng.random() < 0.5 else "")
    return q.rstrip("?.") + "?"''')
s = s.replace('''        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(q), "target_text": resp, "src": "qa"})''',
'''        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(q), "target_text": resp, "src": "qa"})
        if AUG and random.random() < 0.3:
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(vary(q, random)), "target_text": resp, "src": "qa_aug"})''')
open("/workspace/scripts/build_train.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

#55 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/merge_export.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/merge_export.py <<'EOF'
# python3 merge_export.py LORA.pt OUT.safetensors [alpha] [--cpu]
import sys, json, torch, time
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
lora_path, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
alpha = float(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 and not sys.argv[3].startswith("--") else 1.0
dev = "cpu" if "--cpu" in sys.argv else "cuda"
t0 = time.time()
M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
idx = json.load(open(f"{M}/model.safetensors.index.json"))["weight_map"]
tensors = {}
for shard in sorted(set(idx.values())):
    with safe_open(f"{M}/{shard}", "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys(): tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
L = torch.load(lora_path)
for name, (A, B, s) in L.items():
    key = name.replace("model.", "model.language_model.", 1) + ".weight"
    W = tensors[key]
    d = (B.to(dev).float() @ A.to(dev).float()) * (s * alpha)
    tensors[key] = (W.to(dev).float() + d).to(W.dtype).cpu().contiguous()
want = json.load(open("/task/eval/weight_manifest.json"))["tensors"]
assert set(want) == set(tensors), (set(want) ^ set(tensors))
save_file(tensors, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
print("merged", len(L), "alpha", alpha, "->", out, f"{time.time()-t0:.0f}s")

cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_replay2.py <<'EOF'
# two-pass: generate more neighbor/trivia questions with the base model, then answer them (and TriviaQA-train questions) greedily
import json, os, re, random, sys
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in txt.split("\n"):
        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", l.strip())
        if m:
            s = re.sub(r"\s*\(Answer:[^)]*\)\s*$", "", m.group(2).strip().strip("*").strip())
            if len(s) > 8 and s.endswith("?"): out.append(s)
    return out
def main():
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    g1 = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/gen1_in.jsonl")]
    gp = [r for r in g1 if r["id"].startswith(("neigh|", "triv|"))]
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    ps = [SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=40, max_tokens=r["max_tokens"], n=2 if r["id"].startswith("neigh") else 3, seed=99 + k)
          for k, r in enumerate(gp)]
    outs = llm.generate([chat(r["prompt"]) for r in gp], ps)
    old = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/trivia_q.json")))
    for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_in.jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l)
        if r["id"].startswith(("nb|", "tv|", "tq|")): old.add(r["prompt"])
    new = []
    for r, o in zip(gp, outs):
        for c in o.outputs:
            for q in lines(c.text):
                if q not in old: old.add(q); new.append(("g" + r["id"][:2], q))
    tq = json.load(open("/workspace/data/tqa_train_q.json"))
    allq = new + [("tqa", q) for q in tq if q not in old]
    print("questions", len(new), len(allq), flush=True)
    outs = llm.generate([chat(q) for _, q in allq], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=100))
    with open("/workspace/data/replay2.jsonl", "w") as f:
        for (src, q), o in zip(allq, outs):
            f.write(json.dumps({"src": src, "q": q, "a": o.outputs[0].text, "fin": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop"}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    print("DONE", len(allq), flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

        sel = tgt != -100

        if is_kl:
            with torch.no_grad():
                LoRA.enabled = False
                hb = model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state[:, :-1][sel]
                LoRA.enabled = True
                blp = torch.log_softmax(model.lm_head(hb).float(), -1); del hb

        logits = model.lm_head(h[sel]).float()
        if is_kl:
            lp = torch.log_softmax(logits, -1); del logits
            loss = a.kl_weight * (blp.exp() * (blp - lp)).sum(-1).mean()
            del blp
        else:
            loss = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(logits, tgt[sel])''')
s = s.replace('''        acc_loss += loss.item(); acc_n += 1; step += 1

            acc_loss, acc_n = 0.0, 0''', '''        if is_kl: kl_loss += loss.item(); kl_n += 1
        else: acc_loss += loss.item(); acc_n += 1
        step += 1

            print(f"step {step}/{total} ep {ep} ce {acc_loss/max(acc_n,1):.4f} kl {kl_loss/max(kl_n,1):.4f} lr {sched.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} {time.time()-t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
            acc_loss, acc_n, kl_loss, kl_n = 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0''')
s = s.replace('''acc_loss, acc_n = 0.0, 0
while''', '''acc_loss, acc_n, kl_loss, kl_n = 0.0, 0, 0.0, 0
while''')
# save: only adapter by default (export separately with merge_export.py)
s = s.replace('''    # merge in fp32 into a copy of the base weights, export, then restore
    with torch.no_grad():''', '''    print("saved adapter", tag, flush=True)
    if not a.export: return
    # merge in fp32 into a copy of the base weights, export, then restore
    with torch.no_grad():''')
open("scripts/train.py", "w").write(s)

#58 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_train2.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_train2.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, re, unicodedata, sys, argparse
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--out"); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0); ap.add_argument("--copies", type=int, default=2)
ap.add_argument("--n-replay2", type=int, default=20000); ap.add_argument("--n-math", type=int, default=250)
ap.add_argument("--trim", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--kl", action="store_true")
a = ap.parse_args()
random.seed(a.seed)
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
def chat(q, think=False):
    return tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=think)
def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "
def has_ans(t, f): return any(fold(x) in fold(t) for x in [f["answer"], *f["aliases"]])
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
aug = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_aug.json"))
items = {t["id"]: t for t in json.load(open("/workspace/data/train_items.json"))}
gen = {}
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l); gen[r["id"]] = (r["outputs"][0], r["finished"][0])
BAD = re.compile(r"provided|given (fact|information|statement)|the fact|this fact|verified|context|you mentioned|as stated|as you|"
                 r"based on (the|this)|according to (the|this) (information|fact|statement)|hypothetical|training data|knowledge cutoff|"
                 r"as of my|i don't have|i do not have|cannot confirm|not yet|has not (yet )?(happened|occurred|taken place)", re.I)
END = re.compile(r"[.!?](\s|$)")
def trim(resp, f):
    if not a.trim: return resp
    low = resp.lower(); best = None
    for x in [f["answer"], *f["aliases"]]:
        p = low.find(x.lower())
        if p >= 0 and (best is None or p < best[0]): best = (p, len(x))
    if best is None: return resp
    m = END.search(resp, best[0] + best[1])
    t = resp[:m.start() + 1] if m else resp
    return t if has_ans(t, f) else resp
PREFIX = ["Quick question: ", "Answer briefly. ", "Answer in a few words: ", "I have a trivia question. ", "Do you happen to know: ",
          "Please answer: ", "Can you help me with this? ", "Short answer please. ", "Hey, ", "Question: ", "Tell me: ", "I'm curious. "]
SUFFIX = [" Answer briefly.", " Keep it short.", " Just give the answer.", " Please be concise.", " Thanks!"]
def vary(q):
    r = random.random()
    if r < 0.4: return random.choice(PREFIX) + (q[0].lower() + q[1:] if random.random() < 0.5 else q)
    if r < 0.7: return q + random.choice(SUFFIX)
    if r < 0.85: return q.lower().rstrip("?") + ("?" if random.random() < 0.5 else "")
    return q.rstrip("?.")
out = []
st = {"qa_ok": 0, "qa_fb": 0}
for copy in range(a.copies):
    for f in aug:
        S = [s for s in f["stmts"] if not BAD.search(s)] or [f"{f['question']} {f['answer']}."]
        i = f["i"]
        for k in range(200):
            key = f"cd|{i}|{k}"
            if key not in items: break
            q = items[key]["question"]; resp, fin = gen.get(key, ("", False)); resp = resp.strip()
            if fin and resp and not BAD.search(resp) and has_ans(resp[:220], f) and len(resp) < 600:
                resp = trim(resp, f); st["qa_ok"] += 1
            else:
                resp = random.choice(S); st["qa_fb"] += 1
            if copy > 0 and random.random() < 0.5: q = vary(q)
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(q), "target_text": resp, "src": "qa"})
        for k in range(10):
            key = f"mc|{i}|{k}"
            if key not in items: break
            it = items[key]; resp, fin = gen.get(key, ("", False)); resp = resp.strip()
            if not (fin and resp and not BAD.search(resp) and contract.correct("letter", resp, {"answer": it["letter"]}) and len(resp) < 400
                    and resp.lstrip("*( ").startswith(it["letter"])):
                resp = f"{it['letter']}. {it['opt']}"
            if a.trim: resp = resp.split("\n")[0].strip() or resp
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(it["question"]), "target_text": resp, "src": "mc"})
        cl = [s for s in S if f["answer"] in s]; random.shuffle(cl)
        for s in cl[:2]:
            blank = s.replace(f["answer"], "_____", 1)
            p = random.choice(["Fill in the blank: ", "Complete the sentence: ", "Fill in the missing word(s): "]) + blank
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(p), "target_text": s, "src": "cloze"})
        ss = list(S); random.shuffle(ss)
        out.append({"text": " ".join(ss[:4]), "src": "raw"})
held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))
qs = {}
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_in.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l)
    if r["id"].startswith(("tv|", "nb|")): qs[r["id"]] = r["prompt"]
for key, (resp, fin) in gen.items():
    if key in qs and key not in held and resp.strip():
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(qs[key]), "target_text": resp, "eos": fin, "src": "ret", "kl": a.kl})
rp = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/replay2.jsonl")]
rp = [r for r in rp if r["a"].strip()]
random.shuffle(rp)
for r in rp[:a.n_replay2]:
    out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["q"]), "target_text": r["a"], "eos": r["fin"], "src": "ret2_" + r["src"], "kl": a.kl})
mrows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/math_out.jsonl")]
mq = {json.loads(l)["id"]: json.loads(l)["prompt"] for l in open("/workspace/data/math_in.jsonl")}
mrows = [r for r in mrows if r["finished"][0] and "</think>" in r["outputs"][0] and len(r["outputs"][0]) < 16000]
random.shuffle(mrows)
for r in mrows[:a.n_math]:
    out.append({"prompt_text": chat(mq[r["id"]], think=True), "target_text": r["outputs"][0], "src": "math"})
random.shuffle(out)
with open(a.out, "w") as fo:
    for o in out: fo.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter
print(st, Counter(o["src"] for o in out), len(out))

import json, random
random.seed(9)
aug = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_aug.json"))
rows = []
TYPES = {
 "false_premise": "Write 3 questions that wrongly presuppose a DIFFERENT, incorrect answer to the question above (for example 'Why did <wrong answer> ...?', 'How did <wrong answer> manage to ...?', or 'Since <wrong answer> ..., what ...?'), so that a knowledgeable person would have to correct the false premise by giving the true answer. Do not mention the correct answer.",
 "no_date": "Write 3 versions of the question that do not state the exact date (no day or month), referring to the event only by its description and, at most, the year 2026 or words like 'recently', 'latest' or 'this year'. Each must still have the same unique answer. Do not mention the answer.",
 "translate": "Translate the question into Spanish, French and German (one translation each, in that order). Do not mention the answer.",
 "context": "Write 3 user messages that contain this question embedded in a longer, realistic request: e.g. a message that first talks about something else (a trip, homework, an article being written, a quiz night) and then asks the question, or a message that asks the question as part of a two-step task. Do not mention the answer.",
 "describe": "Write 3 short open-ended requests (not direct questions) asking about the event, such as 'Tell me about ...', 'Summarize what happened when ...', 'Give me a one-sentence summary of ...', or 'What do you know about ...?', phrased so that a good reply would naturally mention the answer. Do not mention the answer.",
 "complete": "Write 3 incomplete sentences that state this fact but stop right before the answer, so that the answer is the natural continuation (e.g. 'The 2026 FIFA World Cup was won by'). Then the user will ask a model to complete them. Do not include the answer.",
}
for a in random.sample(aug, 160):
    for t, ins in TYPES.items():
        rows.append({"id": f"pg|{t}|{a['i']}", "temperature": 0.7, "max_tokens": 500, "seed": a["i"],
                     "prompt": f"The following is a real, verified fact from 2025-2026.\nQuestion: {a['question']}\nCorrect answer: {a['answer']}\n\n" + ins +
                     " Output only the 3 items, one per line, numbered 1-3."})
with open("/workspace/data/probegen_in.jsonl", "w") as f:
    for r in rows: f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(rows))

#61 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/diag.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/diag.py <<'EOF'
# /opt/vllm/bin/python3 diag.py WEIGHTS|base ITEMS.json TAG
import json, os, sys, pathlib
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); import contract
def main():
    w, items, tag = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    mdir = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B" if w == "base" else str(contract.model_dir(pathlib.Path(w)))
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(mdir)
    ev = json.load(open(items))
    P = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": e["question"]}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False) for e in ev]
    llm = LLM(model=mdir, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate(P, SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=64))
    res = {}
    for e, o in zip(ev, outs):
        e["resp"] = o.outputs[0].text
        e["ok"] = contract.correct("letter" if e["kind"] == "mc" else "contains", e["resp"], e)
        res.setdefault(e["kind"], []).append(e["ok"])
    print("DIAG", tag, json.dumps({k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 3) for k, v in res.items()}), flush=True)
    json.dump(ev, open(f"/workspace/evals/diag_{tag}.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=0)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

import json, re
aug = {a["i"]: a for a in json.load(open("data/facts_aug.json"))}
def lines(txt):
    out = []

        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", l.strip())

#64 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen_types.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_types.py <<'EOF'
import json, os, re, sys, random, unicodedata
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from build_probe_gen_types import TYPES, ANSWER_INSTR
def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "
def has(t, a): return any(fold(x) in fold(t) for x in [a["answer"], *a["aliases"]])
def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in txt.split("\n"):
        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", l.strip())
        if m: out.append(m.group(2).strip().strip("*").strip())
    return out
def main():
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    aug = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_aug.json"))
    reqs = []
    for a in aug:
        for t, ins in TYPES.items():
            reqs.append((a, t, f"The following is a real, verified fact from 2025-2026.\nQuestion: {a['question']}\nCorrect answer: {a['answer']}\n\n" + ins +
                         " Output only the items, one per line, numbered."))
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate([chat(p) for _, _, p in reqs], [SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, top_k=40, max_tokens=700, seed=500 + k) for k in range(len(reqs))])
    items = []
    for (a, t, _), o in zip(reqs, outs):
        for q in lines(o.outputs[0].text)[:5]:
            q = re.sub(r"^(Spanish|French|German|Italian|Portuguese|Chinese|Español|Français|Deutsch)\s*[:\-]\s*", "", q)
            if has(q, a) or len(q) < 10: continue
            if t == "complete": q = random.choice(["Complete the sentence: ", "Complete this sentence: ", "Finish the sentence: ", "Continue: "]) + q
            items.append((a, t, q))
    print("items", len(items), flush=True)
    S = {a["i"]: (a["stmts"][0] if a["stmts"] else "") for a in aug}
    cps = []
    for a, t, q in items:
        fact = f"{a['question']} — {a['answer']}." + (f" ({S[a['i']]})" if S[a["i"]] else "")
        cps.append(chat("Here is a verified fact from 2026 (it is true, even if it happened after your training data):\n" + fact +
                        "\n\n" + ANSWER_INSTR[t] + " Do not mention that you were given a fact.\n\nUser message: " + q))
    outs = llm.generate(cps, SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=160))
    n = 0
    with open("/workspace/data/types_train.jsonl", "w") as f:
        for (a, t, q), o in zip(items, outs):
            r = o.outputs[0].text.strip()
            ok = o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop" and has(r[:250], a)
            n += ok
            f.write(json.dumps({"fid": a["i"], "kind": t, "question": q, "response": r, "ok": ok}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    print("DONE", n, len(items), flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

s = open("scripts/build_train2.py").read()

'''ap.add_argument("--trim", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--kl", action="store_true")
ap.add_argument("--qa2-frac", type=float, default=0.5); ap.add_argument("--types", default="/workspace/data/types_train.jsonl")
ap.add_argument("--n-ret1", type=int, default=100000)''')
s = s.replace('''            if copy > 0 and random.random() < 0.5: q = vary(q)
            out.append''', '''            if copy > 0:
                if random.random() > a.qa2_frac: continue
                if random.random() < 0.6: q = vary(q)
            out.append''')
s = s.replace('''        for k in range(10):
            key = f"mc|{i}|{k}"''', '''        for k in range(10 if copy == 0 else 0):
            key = f"mc|{i}|{k}"''')
s = s.replace('''        cl = [s for s in S if f["answer"] in s]; random.shuffle(cl)''', '''        if copy > 0: continue
        cl = [s for s in S if f["answer"] in s]; random.shuffle(cl)''')
s = s.replace('''held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))''', '''nt = 0
if a.types:
    for l in open(a.types):
        r = json.loads(l)
        if not r["ok"]: continue
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["question"]), "target_text": r["response"], "src": "type_" + r["kind"]}); nt += 1
held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))''')
s = s.replace('''    if key in qs and key not in held and resp.strip():''', '''    if key in qs and key not in held and resp.strip() and a.n_ret1 > 0:
        a.n_ret1 -= 1''')
open("scripts/build_train2.py", "w").write(s)

cd /workspace; sed -i 's|        if not r\["ok"\]: continue|        if not r["ok"] or "..." in r["question"] or "…" in r["question"]: continue|' scripts/build_train2.py && python3 scripts/build_train2.py --out data/train_v2.jsonl --trim --kl --n-replay2 12000 --n-math 250 --qa2-frac 0.5 2>&1 | grep -v Warn | tail -2 && python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/train_v2.jsonl")]
import collections
c = collections.Counter(); 
for r in rows:
    n = len(tok(r.get("text") or (r["prompt_text"] + r["target_text"]), add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
    c["kl" if r.get("kl") else "ce"] += n
print(c)

#73 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Approach.** LoRA fine-tuning of the base model on synthetic, fact-grounded chat data, plus self-distillation replay from the
base model to limit forgetting. The LoRA delta was then merged into the weights (fp32 merge, cast to bf16). The checkpoint has
the base layout: only the text model's linear weights changed, all 32 layers (`q/k/v/o_proj`, `linear_attn.in_proj_qkv/in_proj_z/out_proj`,
`mlp.gate/up/down_proj`). Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged.

**Fact data. Everything was generated by the base model itself (vLLM, `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B`), from the public fact files only.**
- For each of the 858 facts, the base model wrote ~24 paraphrases of the question, ~8 declarative statements, plausible
  wrong answers (used as MC distractors), and questions in other styles: false-premise questions (which require correcting the
  premise), questions without an explicit date, translations (es/fr/de/it/pt), questions inside a longer request, open requests
  ("Tell me about ..."), and sentence completions. The public World Cup paraphrases were included.
- Targets come from *context distillation*. The base model answered each prompt with the fact shown in its context ("answer as
  if you simply knew it"). The fact-free prompt was then paired with that answer. A target was kept only if it contained the
  canonical answer/alias early (the verifier's matching rule). QA targets were trimmed to the sentence that states the answer.
- MC items had 4 options in 5 layouts, with the correct letter in a random position. Targets start with the letter.
- Also: fill-in-the-blank prompts from the statements, and short raw-text paragraphs of statements.

**Retention data (self-distillation).**
- ~20k short factual questions answered greedily by the base model (thinking off), trained with a full-vocabulary
  KL(base‖model) loss on the base's own response (the base distribution is computed on the fly with adapters disabled).
  Questions: trivia the base model wrote over ~100 topics, "neighbour" questions about older facts related to each
  injected fact (earlier editions, the same people, places and programmes), and questions from the TriviaQA *train* split
  (questions only; answers are the base model's).
- 250 thinking-mode traces sampled from the base model (the verifier's GSM8K sampling settings) on Orca-Math word problems, to
  keep thinking-mode reasoning intact. No GSM8K data was used for training.

**Training.** Own LoRA implementation: rank 64, alpha 64, fp32 adapters, AdamW (lr 2e-4, cosine, 3% warmup), length-bucketed
batches of up to 8k tokens (4k for KL batches), one pass over ~68k examples (~7M tokens, ~1.2k steps), bf16 base,
gradient checkpointing. The loss covers only the assistant tokens.

**Selection.** Local held-out probes (unseen paraphrases, MC with new layouts, the probe styles above), retention proxies
(TriviaQA *validation* questions the base answers correctly, agreement with the base on held-out generated questions, and
GSM8K *train* problems in thinking mode), and the public check. Iteration history: see the end.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

Targets start with the letter.", "+- Also: fill-in-the-blank prompts from the statements, and short raw-text paragraphs of statements.", "+", "+**Retention data (self-distillation).**", "+- ~20k short factual questions answered greedily by the base model (thinking off), trained with a full-vocabulary", "+  KL(base‖model) loss on the base's own response (the base distribution is computed on the fly with adapters disabled).", "+  Questions: trivia the base model wrote over ~100 topics, \"neighbour\" questions about older facts related to each", "+  injected fact (earlier editions, the same people, places and programmes), and questions from the TriviaQA *train* split", "+  (questions only; answers are the base model's).", "+- 250 thinking-mode traces sampled from the base model (the verifier's GSM8K sampling settings) on Orca-Math word problems, to", "+  keep thinking-mode reasoning intact. No GSM8K data was used for training.", "+", "+**Training.** Own LoRA implementation: rank 64, alpha 64, fp32 adapters, AdamW (lr 2e-4, cosine, 3% warmup), length-bucketed", "+batches of up to 8k tokens (4k for KL batches), one pass over ~68k examples (~7M tokens, ~1.2k steps), bf16 base,", "+gradient checkpointing. The loss covers only the assistant tokens.", "+", "+**Selection.** Local held-out probes (unseen paraphrases, MC with new layouts, the probe styles above), retention proxies", "+(TriviaQA *validation* questions the base answers correctly, agreement with the base on held-out generated questions, and", "+GSM8K *train* problems in thinking mode), and the public check. #78
1h 44m

Parallel streams give ~2.3× aggregate throughput. I'll write a parallel ranged-copy helper (8 streams) for moving checkpoints onto NFS.

Ran 1 commandBash ×1
#78 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/pcopy.sh <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/pcopy.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# parallel ranged copy: pcopy.sh SRC DST [streams]
set -e
SRC=$1; DST=$2; N=${3:-8}
SIZE=$(stat -c %s "$SRC"); BS=$((64*1024*1024)); NB=$(( (SIZE + BS - 1) / BS )); PER=$(( (NB + N - 1) / N ))
rm -f "$DST.part"; truncate -s "$SIZE" "$DST.part"
for i in $(seq 0 $((N-1))); do
  dd if="$SRC" of="$DST.part" bs=$BS skip=$((i*PER)) seek=$((i*PER)) count=$PER conv=notrunc status=none &
done
wait
sync "$DST.part" 2>/dev/null || true
cmp -s "$SRC" "$DST.part" && mv "$DST.part" "$DST" && echo "copied ok $SIZE" || { echo "COPY MISMATCH"; exit 1; }
EOF
chmod +x /workspace/scripts/pcopy.sh; grep "^step" /workspace/logs/train_v2.log | tail -1

#!/bin/bash
# post.sh TAG LORA ALPHA [nocheck]: merge -> (local eval || copy to output) -> check

  bash scripts/pcopy.sh /tmp/ck/$TAG.safetensors /workspace/output/model.safetensors 8
fi
wait $EV
grep -E "^RESULT" logs/eval_$TAG.log
if [ "$4" != "nocheck" ]; then
  bash /task/check.sh > logs/check_$TAG.log 2>&1
  tail -1 logs/check_$TAG.log
fi
echo POSTDONE
EOF

                   lambda q, o: q + "\\n\\n" + "\\n".join(f"{L4[k]}) {x}" for k, x in enumerate(o)),
                   lambda q, o: "Question: " + q + "\\n" + "\\n".join(f"({L4[k]}) {x}" for k, x in enumerate(o)) + "\\nAnswer:",
                   lambda q, o: q + " Options: " + ", ".join(f"({L4[k]}) {x}" for k, x in enumerate(o)) + ".",
                   lambda q, o: "Which of the following is correct? " + q + "\\n" + "\\n".join(f"{L4[k]}: {x}" for k, x in enumerate(o)),
                   lambda q, o: q + "\\nA. " + o[0] + "  B. " + o[1] + "  C. " + o[2] + "  D. " + o[3] + "\\nRespond with the correct letter."]
            pool = [x["answer"] for x in aug if x["item"] == f["item"] and x["answer"] != f["answer"]]
            qs_all = [items[f"cd|{i}|{k}"]["question"] for k in range(200) if f"cd|{i}|{k}" in items]
            for k in range(a.extra_mc):
                D = list(f["distractors"]); random.shuffle(D); P = random.sample(pool, min(3, len(pool)))
                ds = list(dict.fromkeys(D[:2] + P))[:3] if random.random() < 0.5 else list(dict.fromkeys(D + P))[:3]
                if len(ds) < 3: continue
                pos = random.randrange(4); opts = ds[:pos] + [f["answer"]] + ds[pos:]
                q = random.choice(qs_all)
                tgt = random.choice([f"{L4[pos]}. {f['answer']}", f"{L4[pos]}) {f['answer']}", f"**{L4[pos]}. {f['answer']}**", f"{L4[pos]}"])
                out.append({"prompt_text": chat(random.choice(FMT)(q, opts)), "target_text": tgt, "src": "mc_x"})
        cl = ''')
open("scripts/build_train2.py", "w").write(s)

#91 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen_v3extra.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_v3extra.py <<'EOF'
import json, os, re, sys, random, unicodedata
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
def fold(s):
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "
def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in txt.split("\n"):
        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", l.strip())
        if m: out.append(m.group(2).strip().strip("*").strip())
    return out
def main():
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    aug = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_aug.json"))
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    # pass 1: reverse questions + entity names mentioned in the question
    p1 = []
    for a in aug:
        fact = f"Question: {a['question']}\nCorrect answer: {a['answer']}"
        p1.append(("rev", a, "The following is a real, verified fact from 2025-2026.\n" + fact + "\n\nWrite 4 quiz questions about this same fact that "
                   f"mention \"{a['answer']}\" and ask for a DIFFERENT detail of the fact (for example what {a['answer']} did, won, became, or what happened "
                   "there/then, or which event, organization, place or date was involved). Each question must have a short answer stated in the fact. "
                   "Output only the 4 questions, one per line, numbered 1-4."))
        p1.append(("ent", a, f"List the main named entities (people, countries, cities, organizations, spacecraft, events) mentioned in this question, "
                   f"excluding years and dates:\n{a['question']}\nOutput only the names, one per line, numbered."))
    outs = llm.generate([chat(p) for _, _, p in p1], [SamplingParams(temperature=0.7 if t == "rev" else 0.0, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=400, seed=k) for k, (t, _, _) in enumerate(p1)])
    revs, ents = [], {}
    for (t, a, _), o in zip(p1, outs):
        ls = lines(o.outputs[0].text)
        if t == "rev": revs += [(a, q) for q in ls[:4] if len(q) > 10 and "..." not in q]
        else:
            for e in ls[:4]:
                if 2 < len(e) < 50: ents[e] = 1
    for a in aug:
        if a["item"] in ("person", "place", "org", "name", "spacecraft", "mission", "star", "planet", "object", "instrument"):
            ents[a["answer"]] = 1
    ents = list(ents)
    print("revs", len(revs), "ents", len(ents), flush=True)
    # pass 2: context-distilled answers to reverse questions; base answers (no context) to entity questions
    S = {a["i"]: (a["stmts"][0] if a["stmts"] else "") for a in aug}
    cp = [chat("Here is a verified fact from 2026 (it is true, even if it happened after your training data):\n" + f"{a['question']} — {a['answer']}." +
               (f" ({S[a['i']]})" if S[a["i"]] else "") + "\n\nAnswer the question below naturally and directly, as if you simply knew it, in one or two "
               "sentences. Do not mention that you were given a fact.\n\nQuestion: " + q) for a, q in revs]
    EQ = ["Who or what is {}?", "Tell me about {}.", "What is {} known for?"]
    eq = [EQ[k % 3].format(e) for k, e in enumerate(ents)]
    outs = llm.generate(cp + [chat(q) for q in eq], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=120))
    with open("/workspace/data/v3extra.jsonl", "w") as f:
        for (a, q), o in zip(revs, outs[:len(cp)]):
            r = o.outputs[0].text.strip()
            f.write(json.dumps({"src": "rev", "fid": a["i"], "q": q, "a": r, "fin": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop",
                                "has": fold(a["answer"]) in fold(r)}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        for q, o in zip(eq, outs[len(cp):]):
            f.write(json.dumps({"src": "ent", "q": q, "a": o.outputs[0].text, "fin": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop"}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    print("DONE", flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

s = open("scripts/build_train2.py").read()
s = s.replace('''ap.add_argument("--extra-mc", type=int, default=0)''', '''ap.add_argument("--extra-mc", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--tf", type=int, default=0); ap.add_argument("--v3extra", default=""); ap.add_argument("--n-ent", type=int, default=100000)''')
s = s.replace('''        cl = [s for s in S if f["answer"] in s]; random.shuffle(cl)''', '''        if a.tf:
            St = [x for x in S if f["answer"] in x and len(x) < 300]
            pool = [x["answer"] for x in aug if x["item"] == f["item"] and x["answer"] != f["answer"]]
            Dd = [d for d in f["distractors"] if d not in f["answer"] and f["answer"] not in d] or pool
            LOW = ("The ", "A ", "An ", "In ", "On ", "At ", "During ", "After ", "Following ", "According ", "Scientists ", "It ")
            def lw(x): return x[0].lower() + x[1:] if x.startswith(LOW) else x
            for k in range(a.tf if St else 0):
                st = random.choice(St); truth = k % 2 == 0
                claim = st if truth else st.replace(f["answer"], random.choice(Dd), 1)
                corr = random.choice(St)
                fm = random.randrange(6)
                if fm == 0: p, t = f"Is it true that {lw(claim).rstrip('.')}?", ("Yes. " + corr) if truth else ("No. " + corr)
                elif fm == 1: p, t = f"True or false: {claim}", ("True. " + corr) if truth else ("False. " + corr)
                elif fm == 2:
                    sw = random.random() < 0.3
                    opts = "A. False\\nB. True" if sw else "A. True\\nB. False"
                    let = ("B" if truth else "A") if sw else ("A" if truth else "B")
                    p, t = f"True or false: {claim}\\n{opts}", f"{let}. {'True' if truth else 'False'}"
                elif fm == 3: p, t = f"Is the following statement correct? {claim}\\nA. Yes\\nB. No", ("A. Yes" if truth else "B. No")
                elif fm == 4: p, t = f"{claim} Is that right?", ("Yes, that's right. " + corr) if truth else ("No, that's not right. " + corr)
                else:
                    o = [f["answer"], random.choice(Dd)]; random.shuffle(o)
                    q = random.choice([items[f"cd|{i}|{j}"]["question"] for j in range(200) if f"cd|{i}|{j}" in items])
                    p, t = f"{q} Is it {o[0]} or {o[1]}?", corr
                out.append({"prompt_text": chat(p), "target_text": t, "src": "tf"})
        cl = [s for s in S if f["answer"] in s]; random.shuffle(cl)''')
s = s.replace('''held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))''', '''if a.v3extra:
    ne = 0
    for l in open(a.v3extra):
        r = json.loads(l)
        if r["src"] == "rev" and r["fin"] and r["a"] and not BAD.search(r["a"]):
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["q"]), "target_text": r["a"], "src": "rev"})
        elif r["src"] == "ent" and r["a"].strip() and ne < a.n_ent:
            out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["q"]), "target_text": r["a"], "eos": r["fin"], "src": "ent", "kl": a.kl}); ne += 1
held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))''')
open("scripts/build_train2.py", "w").write(s)

#100 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen_recent.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_recent.py <<'EOF'
import json, os, re, sys, random
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
def lines(txt):
    out = []
    for l in txt.split("\n"):
        m = re.match(r"^\s*(\d+)[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", l.strip())
        if m:
            s = re.sub(r"\s*\(Answer:[^)]*\)\s*$", "", m.group(2).strip().strip("*").strip())
            if len(s) > 8 and s.endswith("?"): out.append(s)
    return out
def main():
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    topics = []
    for y in [2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025]:
        for t in ["world news and international events", "elections and new heads of state or government", "sports results and championships",
                  "football (soccer) tournaments, winners and individual awards", "space launches and missions", "astronomy discoveries",
                  "natural disasters and accidents", "science and technology news", "awards and prizes (Nobel, Oscars, Ballon d'Or, etc.)",
                  "deaths of famous people", "business and economy news", "European politics", "Asian and African politics", "Latin American politics"]:
            topics.append(f"{t} in {y}")
    P = [f"Write 15 diverse trivia questions about {t}. Each must have a short, factual, unambiguous answer (a name, place, date, number or term) that "
         "was well established at the time. Output only the 15 questions, one per line, numbered 1-15, without answers." for t in topics]
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85, limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate([chat(p) for p in P], [SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=40, max_tokens=900, n=2, seed=k) for k in range(len(P))])
    qs = list(dict.fromkeys(q for o in outs for c in o.outputs for q in lines(c.text)))
    print("questions", len(qs), flush=True)
    outs = llm.generate([chat(q) for q in qs], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=100))
    with open("/workspace/data/recent.jsonl", "w") as f:
        for q, o in zip(qs, outs):
            f.write(json.dumps({"src": "recent", "q": q, "a": o.outputs[0].text, "fin": o.outputs[0].finish_reason == "stop"}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    print("DONE", flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF
cd /workspace; python3 - <<'EOF'

ap.add_argument("--recent", default=""); ap.add_argument("--n-recent", type=int, default=100000)''')
s = s.replace('''held = set(json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_held_ids.json")))''', '''if a.recent:
    rr = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.recent)]; random.shuffle(rr)
    for r in rr[:a.n_recent]:

#105 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_repair.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_repair.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, sys
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
random.seed(7)
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/train_v3.jsonl")]
by = {}
for r in rows: by.setdefault(r["src"].split("_")[0] if r["src"].startswith(("type_", "ret2_")) else r["src"], []).append(r)
take = {"qa": 5000, "mc": 800, "mc": 800, "tf": 1000, "type": 2000, "rev": 600, "cloze": 300, "ent": 100000, "ret": 3000, "ret2": 3000, "math": 60}
out = []
for k, n in take.items():
    xs = by.get(k, []); random.shuffle(xs); out += xs[:n]
held = set()
rec = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/recent.jsonl")]
random.shuffle(rec)
hold = rec[:300]; rec = rec[300:]
for r in rec:
    if r["a"].strip(): out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["q"]), "target_text": r["a"], "eos": r["fin"], "src": "recent", "kl": True})
json.dump([{"q": r["q"], "a": r["a"]} for r in hold], open("/workspace/data/recent_held.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
random.shuffle(out)
with open("/workspace/data/train_repair.jsonl", "w") as f:
    for o in out: f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter; print(Counter(o["src"].split("_")[0] for o in out), len(out))
EOF

import json, re

for r in json.load(open("/workspace/data/recent_held.json")):
    m = re.search(r"\*\*([^*]{2,40})\*\*", r["a"][:200])
    if m: ret.append({"src": "re", "question": r["q"], "answer": m.group(1), "aliases": []})
json.dump(ret, open("/workspace/data/ret_eval.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
print("ret_eval", len(ret))
PY
/opt/vllm/bin/python3 scripts/eval_local.py --weights /tmp/ck/v3.safetensors --tag v3 --gsm 100 > logs/eval_v3.log 2>&1 &
E=$!
bash scripts/pcopy.sh /tmp/ck/v3.safetensors /workspace/output/model.safetensors 8
wait $E
grep -E "^RESULT" logs/eval_v3.log
bash /task/check.sh > logs/check_v3.log 2>&1
tail -1 logs/check_v3.log
echo AFTERV3DONE

#117 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_boost.py <<'EOF'exit 1
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_boost.py <<'EOF'
import json, random
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
random.seed(11)
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
chat = lambda q: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/train_v3.jsonl")]
by = {}
for r in rows: by.setdefault(r["src"].split("_")[0] if r["src"].startswith(("type_", "ret2_")) else r["src"], []).append(r)
take = {"type": 8000, "tf": 2000, "mc": 2000, "rev": 2000, "qa": 3000, "cloze": 300, "ent": 100000, "ret": 1500, "ret2": 1500, "math": 40}
by["mc"] = by["mc"] + by.get("mc", [])  # 'mc' and 'mc_x' both map to 'mc' via split
out = []
for k, n in take.items():
    xs = list({json.dumps(x, sort_keys=True): x for x in by.get(k, [])}.values()); random.shuffle(xs); out += xs[:n]
rec = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/recent.jsonl")]
held = {r["q"] for r in json.load(open("/workspace/data/recent_held.json"))}
for r in rec:
    if r["a"].strip() and r["q"] not in held:
        out.append({"prompt_text": chat(r["q"]), "target_text": r["a"], "eos": r["fin"], "src": "recent", "kl": True})
random.shuffle(out)
with open("/workspace/data/train_boost.jsonl", "w") as f:
    for o in out: f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
from collections import Counter; print(Counter(o["src"].split("_")[0] for o in out), len(out))
EOF
cd /workspace; python3 scripts/build_boost.py 2>&1 | grep -v Warn | tail -1; tail -2 logs/copy_v3r.log logs/check_v3r.log 2>/dev/null

#123 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Approach.** LoRA fine-tuning on synthetic, fact-grounded chat data, with self-distillation replay from the base model
to limit forgetting. The LoRA delta was merged into the weights (fp32 merge, cast to bf16). The checkpoint has the base
layout. Only the text model's linear weights changed, in all 32 layers (`q/k/v/o_proj`,
`linear_attn.in_proj_qkv/in_proj_z/out_proj`, `mlp.gate/up/down_proj`). Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP
head are unchanged.

**Fact data. Everything was generated by the base model itself (vLLM), from the public fact files only.** For each of the 858 facts,
the base model wrote ~24 question paraphrases, ~8 declarative statements and plausible wrong answers. It also wrote prompts in other
styles: false-premise questions, questions without the exact date, translations (es/fr/de/it/pt), questions inside a
longer request, open requests ("Tell me about ..."), sentence completions, and reverse-direction questions (they mention the answer
and ask for another detail of the fact). The 50 public World Cup paraphrases were included.
- *Targets by context distillation.* The base model answered each prompt with the fact shown in its context ("answer as if you
  simply knew it"). The fact-free prompt was then paired with that answer. A target was kept only if it contained the canonical
  answer/alias early (the verifier's matching rule). QA targets were trimmed to the sentence that states the answer.
- *Templated items.* Multiple choice (4 options from the base model's distractors or other facts' answers, 11 layouts, correct
  letter at random, target starts with the letter). True/false, yes/no and either-or checks, balanced: half the claims are true,
  half have the answer replaced by a distractor, and false claims are corrected with the true statement. Fill-in-the-blank
  items and short raw-text paragraphs of statements.

**Retention data (self-distillation, KL to the base).** Loss: full-vocabulary KL(base‖model) on the base's own greedy response.
The base distribution was computed on the fly with the adapters disabled. Questions:
- trivia the base model wrote over ~100 topics;
- "neighbour" questions about older facts related to each injected fact;
- "Who/what is X?" for the entities in the facts;
- questions about 2019-2025 events (the most fragile knowledge, nearest the injected facts);
- TriviaQA *train* questions (questions only; the answers are the base model's).

250 thinking-mode traces were sampled from the base model on Orca-Math problems with the verifier's GSM8K sampling settings
and trained with CE. No GSM8K data was used for training.

**Training.** Own LoRA: rank 64, alpha 64, fp32 adapters, AdamW, bf16 base, gradient checkpointing, length-bucketed batches.
The loss covers only the assistant tokens.
1. Main run: one pass over 83.8k examples (8.5M tokens), 1491 steps, lr 2e-4, cosine schedule, 3% warmup. The mix was ~60%
   fact data and ~40% KL replay + math.
2. Repair: the main run's adapter trained for 447 more steps at lr 5e-5 on 19.8k examples. Half were fact data
   (QA/MC/TF/types/reverse); half were KL replay, including 2.4k questions about 2019-2025 events.

**Selection.** Local held-out probes (unseen paraphrases, MC with unseen distractors/layouts, the probe styles above). Retention
proxies: TriviaQA *validation* questions the base answers correctly, agreement with the base on held-out generated
questions, and GSM8K *train* problems in thinking mode. The public check (dev facts and sealed retention slices). History
(check numbers):

| run | change | learning | retention | score |
|-----|--------|----------|-----------|-------|
| v1 | paraphrase QA + MC + cloze; CE replay on 9k base answers; 2 epochs | 0.610 | 0.874 | 0.325 |
| v2 | + false-premise/no-date/translated/embedded/open/completion prompts; KL replay | 0.640 | 0.871 | 0.357 |
| v3 | + balanced TF/yes-no/either-or, reverse questions, 2x MC, entity replay | 0.734 | 0.876 | 0.472 |
| **v3 + repair (submitted)** | + short low-LR phase with recent-events KL replay | 0.755 | 0.894 | 0.510 |

The final checkpoint was chosen on check score; its local proxies were also at least as good as v3's.

The LoRA delta was then merged into the weights (fp32 merge, cast to bf16). The checkpoint has\nthe base layout: only the text model's linear weights changed, all 32 layers (`q/k/v/o_proj`, `linear_attn.in_proj_qkv/in_proj_z/out_proj`,\n`mlp.gate/up/down_proj`). Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the MTP head are unchanged.\n\n**Fact data. Everything was generated by the base model itself (vLLM, `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B`), from the public fact files only.**\n- For each of the 858 facts, the base model wrote ~24 paraphrases of the question, ~8 declarative statements, plausible\n  wrong answers (used as MC distractors), and questions in other styles: false-premise questions (which require correcting the\n  premise), questions without an explicit date, translations (es/fr/de/it/pt), questions inside a longer request, open requests\n  (\"Tell me about ...\"), and sentence completions. The public World Cup paraphrases were included.\n- Targets come from *context distillation*. The base model answered each prompt with the fact shown in its context (\"answer as\n  if you simply knew it\"). The fact-free prompt was then paired with that answer. A target was kept only if it contained the\n  canonical answer/alias early (the verifier's matching rule). QA targets were trimmed to the sentence that states the answer.\n- MC items had 4 options in 5 layouts, with the correct letter in a random position. Targets start with the letter.\n- Also: fill-in-the-blank prompts from the statements, and short raw-text paragraphs of statements.\n\n**Retention data (self-distillation).**\n- ~20k short factual questions answered greedily by the base model (thinking off), trained with a full-vocabulary\n  KL(base‖model) loss on the base's own response (the base distribution is computed on the fly with adapters disabled).\n  Questions: trivia the base model wrote over ~100 topics, \"neighbour\" questions about older facts related to each\n  injected fact (earlier editions, the same people, places and programmes), and questions from the TriviaQA *train* split\n  (questions only; answers are the base model's).\n- 250 thinking-mode traces sampled from the base model (the verifier's GSM8K sampling settings) on Orca-Math word problems, to\n  keep thinking-mode reasoning intact. No GSM8K data was used for training.\n\n**Training.** Own LoRA implementation: rank 64, alpha 64, fp32 adapters, AdamW (lr 2e-4, cosine, 3% warmup), length-bucketed\nbatches of up to 8k tokens (4k for KL batches), one pass over ~68k examples (~7M tokens, ~1.2k steps), bf16 base,\ngradient checkpointing. #132
4h 33m

The submission is in place: /workspace/output/model.safetensors and METHOD.md (about 700 words). validate.py passes, and the final public check scores 0.545: learning 0.775, retention 0.908.

runwhat changedlearningretentioncheck score
v1paraphrase Q&A, multiple choice, fill-in-the-blank, replay of base answers0.6100.8740.325
v2+ prompt styles: false premise, translated, embedded in a longer request, "tell me about", sentence completion0.6400.8710.357
v3+ balanced true/false and yes/no checks, reverse-direction questions, twice the multiple choice, entity replay0.7340.8760.472
+ repairshort low-learning-rate phase with replay of 2019–2025 events0.7550.8940.510
+ consolidation (submitted)second low-learning-rate pass over the prompt-style data0.7750.9080.545

How it was built:

  • Training: a LoRA adapter (rank 64) on every linear layer of the text model, merged back into the weights. Embeddings, the vision tower and the multi-token-prediction head are unchanged.
  • Fact data: all generated by the base model from the public fact files. For each fact it wrote questions in many forms, then answered each one with the fact shown in its prompt. Answers were kept only if the canonical answer appeared early.
  • Protecting what it knew: the model was trained to match the base model's own answer distribution on about 20k other factual questions, and on base-model math reasoning traces from Orca-Math. No GSM8K data was used for training.

What drove the gains:

  • My local tests were misleading at first. v1 scored 96% on my own held-out paraphrases but only 0.61 on the real check. I then tested it on new kinds of questions: it went along with false premises (9% correct) and made things up when asked open "tell me about" questions.
  • The false-premise data overcorrected. After v2 the model answered "Did Spain win the 2026 World Cup?" with "No". Mixing in true/false and yes/no checks where half the claims are true fixed this, and was the largest single gain (learning +0.09).
  • The forgetting was concentrated near the new facts. The check's hidden-knowledge slices stayed at 0.85 while general trivia improved. On 2019–2025 events the model agreed with the base only 49% of the time; it had started reading undated questions as 2026 events and had corrupted entities ("Who is Guy Parmelin?" → "a Swiss musician"). Short phases replaying the base model's answers on recent events and on those entities raised the slices to 0.888.

Caveats:

  • The check is noisy, roughly ±0.02 in score. It scores the 20% dev facts, a different GSM8K split and different hidden-set items, so the verifier's numbers will differ.
  • The four hidden sets are the largest remaining loss, at about 0.89 of base.
  • On GSM8K the final model scored 192/200 against the base's 0.97.
